Best checkpoint uses epoch 28/update 6272 (FDE 0.62254285m); last epoch 30 FDE 0.62330194m, ADE 0.31719040m. FDE difference <1mm; last has slightly better ADE and lateral MAE. These are effectively tied, not evidence of meaningful driving superiority.

Training L1 final-epoch average 0.20285180; single final update 0.05412425; fixed evaluation L1 0.17907640. Do not equate these or interpret them as lateral-only errors.

CCDP epoch30 mean Gumbel retention 32.0406%, p_keep mean 32.0533%, std 0.392536. Argmax evaluation retention 31.4687%, 33.3553 tokens/frame, range22?46. Last dashboard token count37.5 is only the final batch. Increased probability spread does not prove semantic importance selection.

Tail error comparison, old->new: FDE p95 3.4983->2.1674m; final lateral MAE p95 1.0726->0.5305m; FDE>1m windows119->62; final lateral>0.5m windows57->25. Max FDE16.3954->16.4365m: large failures remain.

The worst final-model anchor is frame1054, route progress450.896m, current speed0.224m/s. Recorded future forward distance at2.5s is16.7898m; prediction0.3574m. Frames1044/1049 at the same location are nearly stopped and have large start-up prediction errors; frame1059 at2.881m/s predicts21.375m vsGT21.6629m and improves greatly. Frame350 (route196.312m) is also stopped: GT10.0799m vs prediction1.9922m. This establishes a restart/acceleration failure pattern at these inspected anchors, not its cause. Signal visibility, driver intent, input separability and encoder information are not yet independently tested.

Original first-straight concern: six anchors with frame<90 and GT-straight criterion have ADE0.24707->0.16046m; final lateral MAE0.38960->0.27903m. Criterion: all future positions forward, final forward>3m, max |lateral|/max(forward,1)<.0875. This is a small selected subset, not all starting-route positions.

Restarted optimizer/LR was1e-5 after prior near-zero LR. Fixed evaluation worsens at epoch1 (ADE0.89584) versus starting0.52745; at epoch15 ADE0.51953 and FDE0.98658. Thus some early compute is spent recovering prior accuracy. A lower restart LR is a candidate for any further training, but not a causally proven fix and no change applied.

Conclusion: additional training improved recorded-route fitting by ~40% ADE and ~36% FDE, but does not prove live path tracking or explain away residual errors as insufficient epochs. Next priority is stopped-to-moving cases and live replay. Live Gumbel/instruction-history conditions differ from argmax training-anchor evaluation.

## Stop-to-start exclusion analysis and training settings

This is a diagnostic partition of final epoch30 predictions, not removal from training and not confirmed traffic-light labels. Criterion: current recorded speed <0.5m/s AND recorded displacement at2.5s >3m. Selected14/447 overlapping anchors (3.13%). Excluding these gives433 anchors: ADE0.269193m vs0.317190m (~15.1% decrease); FDE0.504967m vs0.623302m (~19.0% decrease); final lateral MAE0.174793m vs0.172716m (slightly worse). The14 anchors contribute17.79% of summed ADE and21.52% of summed FDE. These results do not support attributing most remaining lateral/path error to traffic signals.

Sensitivity: speed<1m/s and future displacement>3m excludes17 anchors, remaining ADE0.270254m/FDE0.508374m. Excluding all speed<0.5m/s anchors excludes43, remaining ADE0.273685m/FDE0.505210m. Excluding just previously inspected frames350/1044/1049/1054 leaves443, ADE0.282172m/FDE0.535912m; not a fair traffic-light-filtered benchmark.

Hypothesis only: encoder traffic-signal representation could affect launch decisions. Alternatives include signal visibility/crop, timing ambiguity before driver acceleration, and downstream use of signal information. Signal state and encoder errors have not been measured in this analysis. Aggregate metrics must remain reported alongside any subset.

Current settings: initial LR1e-5 cosine-decays almost to0 with no warmup; AdamW weight_decay0.06; batch2;30epochs; global gradient clipping10 fixed in utils/trainer.py. Evidence for lower restart LR: before ADE0.52745, epoch1 ADE0.89584, epoch3 ADE1.0404; first ADE recovery below starting point at epoch15. This does not independently establish LR causality because optimizer states are also reset.

Candidate next controlled continuation: start from this run's latest/best weights, initial LR3e-6, keep batch/weight_decay/loss coefficients/sampler unchanged, compare fixed evaluation checkpoints every epoch.3e-6 is a proposed test, not measured optimum. Do not silently change LR and weight_decay simultaneously. Current training config still points to the earlier20260920 model-last.pt; source must be deliberately advanced if another continuation is requested. No config or model changes were made for this analysis.
